SLOPCRAFT
Third-party notices for the Windows x86-64 distribution
=====================================================

This inventory includes the crates resolved by Cargo.lock for
x86_64-pc-windows-msvc, including build-time crates. Each crate entry
points to its license and notice texts below. Identical texts are
listed once. Filenames are relative to the published crate unless an
upstream source is marked. The Rust toolchain has a separate inventory.

Bitmap font
-----------

The interface uses the font8x8 0.3.1 Rust crate by Joaquin Rosales.
The crate is licensed under MIT. Its public-domain bitmap glyphs
derive from work credited to Marcel Sondaar and IBM (VGA fonts),
with the C port credited to Daniel Hepper.
Rust crate: https://gitlab.com/saibatizoku/font8x8-rs
C port: https://github.com/dhepper/font8x8

distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
limitations under the License.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

This file, wgl.xml, is the WGL API Registry. The older ".spec" file
format has been retired and will no longer be updated with new
extensions and API versions. The canonical version of the registry,
together with documentation, schema, and Python generator scripts used
to generate C header files for WGL, can always be found in the Khronos
Registry at
        https://github.com/KhronosGroup/OpenGL-Registry
